# Oteryn Game — recurring events gap audit

**Audit date:** 2026-11-01. **Scope:** research/documentation only. **Decision:** Oteryn does not cover the reconciled current Tibia event catalogue. Of **249 distinct current event families**, **DONE: 1**, **PARTIAL: 25**, and **MISSING: 223** under the execution-based definitions below. This is not a count of missing monster assets or a claim that every private quest cooldown is a separate event.

## 1. Baselines, access and evidence rules

| Source | Immutable revision / purpose |
|---|---|
| Oteryn latest main inspected | [`38641fecd4558a4a14e6b15b3874110ffa2dff19`](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/commit/38641fecd4558a4a14e6b15b3874110ffa2dff19) |
| Primary donor Crystal | [`201189f871d23597b82caf88fd611454a2ddefb8`](https://github.com/zimbadev/crystalserver/commit/201189f871d23597b82caf88fd611454a2ddefb8) |
| Secondary donor Canary | [`52550d5e3c0ffe565f4bd1014f4e5575abf1059b`](https://github.com/opentibiabr/canary/commit/52550d5e3c0ffe565f4bd1014f4e5575abf1059b) |
| Existing re-audit, read only | [2026-10-28 re-audit at main](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/blob/38641fecd4558a4a14e6b15b3874110ffa2dff19/docs/architecture/reviews/OTERYN_GAME_CRYSTAL_REAUDIT_2026-10-28.md), especially W05a–e, W16, W19 and owner exclusions |
| In-flight overlap | [Draft #2781](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2781), published head `4c33b1409af987aba430bcd3e466c16fa9ca1f0e`; body and discussion inspected. Authoring/test claims for unpublished work do not become main coverage. |

Wiki pages were fetched **only through the two MediaWiki APIs**, with `Oteryn-Events-Gap-Audit/1.0 (+https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game; research-only)` and one shared request limiter exceeding one second between requests across both sites. Revision ids in the table are the retrieved revisions, not guessed latest ids. Official news/calendar reads used ordinary read-only requests. Direct tibia.com access returned HTTP403; indexed official-source text/metadata was used where available, with exact news ids. The report does not claim successful direct calendar retrieval or a login/bypass. No CipSoft game connection was made.

Positive code claims require an opened implementation and a production caller/registration. Negative findings are bounded to the inspected event owners, content admission, complete script/NPC inventories and opened matching files; a keyword search, empty namespace, monster profile, quest journal or test is not executable event coverage. The repository paths in table links resolve at the pins above. Minified content shards legitimately use line1 plus the named canonical identity.

**DONE** means the bounded recurring mechanic is reachable through the inspected production path and no sourced gap was identified. **PARTIAL** means a reachable part exists but effects, current rules, physical/content admission or qualification remain incomplete. **MISSING** means no qualified production lifecycle for that named event was established, even where imported assets or reusable systems exist. These are source-audit verdicts; no live-client/server gameplay test was performed for this documentation PR.

`#2781=yes` means the named slice is explicitly owned in that draft, not that the whole current-Tibia event is implemented. `partly` means only components or explicit exclusions overlap. All status cells measure pinned main. Wider Prey work belongs to [draft #2797](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2797); only its bounded free-reroll recurrence is assessed here.

### Counting and scope

One row denotes a named world/calendar event, public encounter family or distinct shared recurring system. A multi-stage event keeps its internal phases, reward timers and event-owned raids in its parent row. The three Oramond Factory factions are one hourly vote-selected family; Halloween Pumpkin/Hare, seasonal Dryads/Jesters/Goblins and Cake Golems stay with their parent events. Combined bonus windows and compensation windows configure existing bonus families and are not extra event counts. `daily` includes server-save/random mini-change checks, not a promise of daily activation; `weekly` denotes a seven-day route/cycle; `seasonal` denotes the two separated yearly festival seasons; `annual` denotes a recurring named annual festival, including Autumn's two October windows. Exact unknown cadence is explicitly unverified.

The provisional personal-timer boundary is recorded in Q1, not an accepted owner exclusion. Ordinary personal repeatable objectives/chests are not individually enumerated. Retired and one-off historical entries are retained separately. Store payment/advertising is a pending owner boundary (Q3); earned gameplay effects remain included.

| annual | 0 | 1 | 19 | 20 |
| bonus | 0 | 4 | 2 | 6 |
| rotation | 0 | 12 | 167 | 179 |
| **Total** | **1** | **25** | **223** | **249** |

The single DONE result is **Prey free reroll every20h**, a deliberately narrow recurrence claim. It does not certify the rest of Prey. Several useful systems are already live: daily boosts, Weekly Tasks, Daily30 server claims, Rashid placement, continuous day/night NPC placement, an Echo encounter subset and admitted boss-spawn policies. PARTIAL preserves this evidence instead of repeating an outdated blanket absence verdict.

## 3. Production findings that change the verdict

### World changes and physical effects

The main world/mini-change catalogues remain `READY_UNPOPULATED` at `content/encounters/world-changes/index.json:7` and `content/encounters/mini-world-changes/index.json:7`. `crates/gameplay/world-change/src/content.rs:114–137,227–230` requires six unresolved binding gaps; its validated source candidates cannot satisfy `require_w1_bindings`. The boot roller in `crates/gameplay/world-change/src/daily.rs:73–100` computes several flags, but `npcs()` emits only Rosemarie. The production applier in `apps/game-server/src/node/serve/daily_world_changes.rs:37–62` iterates those NPCs. This supports Flower Month PARTIAL; logging a Thawing/Voice/Yasir/Grimvale flag does not implement its map, raid or reward effect.

Seasonal authored quest records also do not establish playable events: the cited shards carry `runtime_enabled=false` or missing native lowering. Hot Cuisine's `content/quests/missions/source-lowered-binding-plan.json:15–26` has48 unbound transitions and zero native bindings. Stan and Valentina **do have imported NPC and trade-service records** (`content/services/trade/trade-00000-00323.json:1`); the missing finding concerns annual placement/entitlement and complete qualified offers, not a claim that those records do not exist.

### Existing systems with concrete current-rule differences

| Mechanic | Sourced difference / consequence |
|---|---|
| Daily boosts | `crates/gameplay/durability/src/durability/daily_boost.rs:52–61` excludes the previous epoch only. Current Boosted Creature oldid927512 and Cyclopedia oldid1197515 specify30 days. Main `crates/gameplay/combat/src/combat/death_reward.rs:931,969,979–1018` reaches one Bosstiary credit; `durability/src/durability/bosstiary.rs:65` adds1, while current boosted boss requires3. Crystal `player.cpp:6563–6600`/config244 and Canary `player.cpp:6661–6696`/config221 already supply3. |
| Weekly Tasks | Main `crates/gameplay/quests/src/quest/task_board_full.rs:325–334` follows Crystal `src/io/ioweeklytasks.cpp:470–486` at5/9/13/17 completions. Current Task Board oldid1187430 and Canary `data/modules/scripts/taskboard/rules.lua:511–525` use4/8/12/16. This is a documented source divergence, not an authorized rule edit in this PR. |
| Double Loot | Main `crates/gameplay/dispatch/src/gameplay_transport/kill_reward.rs:2479–2491` scales a probability and caps it at certainty. Current Double Loot Events oldid1078147 specifies two independent complete loot sets. A guaranteed single drop cannot become two through that probability multiplier. Older BR oldid199841 describes doubled chance and is not used to erase the newer distinction. |
| Bonus stacking | Main `crates/gameplay/foundation/src/scheduled_events.rs:147–204` multiplies schedules. Current XP oldid1211672 describes additive base-XP bonuses; Rapid oldid1199249 has eligibility and additive composition rules. Only the live hit-training consumer is proven by `dispatch/.../attack/skill_training.rs:52–77`; magic/offline/exercise coverage needs separate qualification. |
| Echo Raids | Main lethal-settlement portal queue (`dispatch/.../kill_reward.rs:1184–1200`), movement activation (`paced_movement.rs:158`), polling (`serving.rs:154,185`; `world_respawns.rs:61–88`) and physical actors (`entities/src/carrier/echo_warden.rs:260–285,339–357`) run. The guaranteed Warden plus7–12 minions in `domain/src/domain/echo_warden.rs:3–5,111–154` differs from current BR441496's trigger/outcome families. The120-second portal TTL is not a ten-minute live-child expiry. Catalyst/dust rules are explicitly missing at `:240–246`; news8996 supplies later balance corrections. |
| Raid driver versus boss clock | `apps/game-server/src/node/raid_scheduler.rs:10–31,45–67` admits only `raid:badger` and reports durable creature records. Separately, `content/world/spawns/spawns-boss-spawn1.json:1` and `channel_boot.rs:225–243` feed a real boss clock (`world_respawns.rs:68–195`; `entities/src/carrier/boss_spawns.rs:45–102`). Named admitted boss subsets therefore receive PARTIAL; that does not prove all regional warnings, wave content, access changes or Global cadence. |

These references are expanded to immutable full paths in the event tables. Existing project choices remain distinguishable from accidental gaps: [merged #2746](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2746) adopts Daily30 (merge `809e18146b112f5fa03c9dd47a23927cf6865f24`); [#2753](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2753) establishes the project's06:00 Europe/Berlin save; `docs/agents/tasks/LOOP-MAP-1.md:56–68` deliberately scopes Improved Respawn to one initial area. The audit records remaining Global differences without silently reversing those decisions. [News8998](https://www.tibia.com/news/?subtopic=newsarchive&id=8998) is an October teaser for the **future** Winter2026 update; proposed Daily Reward/Bounty changes are not counted as already-live requirements.

### Donor execution does not imply current Tibia fidelity

Crystal defaults (`config.lua.dist:6,557`) load/start XML raids through `src/game/game.cpp:695–724`, especially711–712. Canary defaults (`config.lua.dist:6,499`) use `src/game/game.cpp:963–992`, especially979–980. Crystal `src/lua/creature/raids.cpp:31–98,106–149,195–257,341–345,391–405` loads children, schedules, broadcasts and physically places creatures. Its `repeat` flag defaults false (`:85–90`); registry `interval2=1440` is not a verified Global daily schedule. All159 active Crystal and62 active Canary XML child entries were opened; none of those referenced files was missing.

Donor examples require explicit correction: Ferumbras spawns after100 seconds versus the sourced20-minute sequence; Orshabaal after60 seconds versus6m40; Canary comments out Ferumbras' XML registry row42. Gaz'haragoth's child only covers the prison placement, not the new-world first-defeat relocation. Canary `rookgaard/rottie1.xml:3–7` actually contains Rats/Cave Rats. Regional rows retain wrong-floor, missing-wave and duplicate Lua registry-name qualifications. The audit does not turn a matching filename into parity.

## 4. Census reconciliation and non-counted schedule forms

World-change discovery combined BR and Fandom API categories:13 BR major world changes plus Steamship and Insectoid Invasion =15. The combined mini-change set is26; Dworc/Hunter Camp is Jungle Camp, and Orc Land is Poacher Caves. Chyllfroest is counted once in annual events. These inventories are supported by [BR World Changes oldid404839](https://www.tibiawiki.com.br/index.php?title=World_Changes&oldid=404839), [BR Mini World Changes oldid436926](https://www.tibiawiki.com.br/index.php?title=Mini_World_Changes&oldid=436926), [Fandom World Changes oldid1050148](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/World_Changes?oldid=1050148), and [Fandom Mini World Changes oldid1002523](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/Mini_World_Changes?oldid=1002523). The calendar/quest discovery also used [Events oldid1024251](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/Events?oldid=1024251), [World Quests oldid838076](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/World_Quests?oldid=838076), [BR World Quests oldid443749](https://www.tibiawiki.com.br/index.php?title=World_Quests&oldid=443749) and [Raid oldid1168721](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/Raid?oldid=1168721).

The16 regional raid pages contain119 event headings: **98 additional current rows +17 aliases of already-counted parents +4 historical headings =119**. This is a reproducible named-event census. It is not evidence that CipSoft publishes every hidden spawn probability, nor that every rare creature is a separate scheduled event. Event-owned seasonal waves and ordinary personal boss cooldowns are handled by the stated boundary; the donor-candidate reconciliation below closes remaining named donor families without inventing exact probabilities.

## 6. Work already owned by #2781

The draft's published head is not on main, and much of its body describes unpublished authoring/validation. The census keeps those events missing/partial on main and marks the overlap. No parallel implementation is proposed for its runtime.

| Existing slice | Follow-up boundary retained by this audit |
|---|---|
| W05b Thawing, Voice, Yasir, Fury Gates, Nightmare Isles, Grimvale, Flower Month, horses | Wait for the existing implementation to merge, then qualify remaining canonical map/NPC/monster/item mappings and complete event-specific leftovers. |
| W05d Cake and calendar | The exact Cake Golem `oteryn:creature.cake_golem` is not admitted; Crystal `data-global/monster/quests/a_piece_of_cake/cake_golem.lua:1,23–28,72–82` has unresolved attack at79. [ANSWER6556536661](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2781#issuecomment-6556536661) forbids alias/health-only substitution. Bridge Item48227 is a separate canonical dependency ([ANSWER6549960114](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2781#issuecomment-6549960114)). The inspected map artifact `3bb1f0f22f3327f95e9e46ca513944c3aeba3d76068e68dc19d5e3b5c5c4997c` has1066 real ground edibles versus the1116 goal:50 missing placements may not be invented or removed from the goal ([ANSWER6549858783](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2781#issuecomment-6549858783)). |
| W05e Autumn | The guarded potion reward is not a playable producer. Berry chance/count, cellar/mould, treading timing, sugar/water and Berrypest trigger/count remain unavailable under [ANSWER6547455508](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2781#issuecomment-6547455508). Source those facts and content before producing completed-load receipts. |
| W19 item actions | Fireworks, costumes, novelty items, Christmas bundles and anniversary boxes are components. They do not establish annual NPC availability, world raids or event entitlements. Missing canonical cosmetic/horse mappings retain refusal before spending under ANSWER6547993529/6548205863. |
| Calendar / Halloween | Christmas/anniversary event windows and the undefined Halloween Hare16:00 raid are explicit leftovers under [ANSWER6547596453](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2781#issuecomment-6547596453). An annual date alone is not a playable event. |
| W16 Phosphorus | Only the sourced curse is owned there; admission, room/party/cooldown and phase transitions remain source gaps under [ANSWER6547535128](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2781#issuecomment-6547535128). A normal quest encounter is not an extra annual-event row. |

## 7. Proposed future PRs

Each numbered group is one proposed PR, not an authorization to implement it in this audit. **S** is a bounded rule/admission correction; **M** extends an existing owner with a coherent mechanic/content slice; **L** introduces a full multi-stage world lifecycle or a substantial qualified map/encounter batch. These are relative scope estimates, not elapsed-time promises. All missing/partial current rows map to one primary group; shared prerequisites are stated separately. G58 is a prerequisite for regional batches. G09/G28/G35 and calendar/item dependencies continue #2781 after merge, without duplicating it.

### G01

**Daily boost rule completion — M.** Persist 30-day exclusion, award three Bosstiary kills, qualify entry reset and bonus composition.

- **Events:** Boosted Creature, Boosted Boss
- **Dependencies:** Prey eligibility, Bestiary difficulty, Archfoe profiles, boss cooldown ownership.
- **Donor versus new rules:** Partial Crystal/Canary; use current Wiki for 30-day history and eligibility.

### G02

**Weekly Task multiplier boundaries — S.** Reconcile intended project rule before changing thresholds; qualify Rookgaard exclusion.

- **Events:** Weekly Tasks
- **Dependencies:** Live Task Board catalogue and settlement.
- **Donor versus new rules:** Canary and current Wiki use 4/8/12/16; Crystal uses 5/9/13/17.

### G03

**Rashid day-boundary relocation — S.** Use one boundary for placement and trade availability.

- **Events:** Rashid route
- **Dependencies:** Seven exact NPC positions, trade service, agreed day boundary.
- **Donor versus new rules:** Both donors have periodic relocation.

### G04

**Day/night actor admission — M.** Qualify all actors; preserve the existing clock owner.

- **Events:** Day/night NPC rotation
- **Dependencies:** All 21 NPC identities, exact positions, dialogue/services.
- **Donor versus new rules:** Both donors supply timed tables; main already has a live loop.

### G05

**Complete the accepted Daily30 client and delivery path — M.** Preserve adopted 30-day behaviour and close unsupported client handlers.

- **Events:** Daily Rewards and monthly reward jokers
- **Dependencies:** Daily reward protocol, native client view/input, canonical rewards and deferred consumers.
- **Donor versus new rules:** Main owner decision #2746 is authoritative; donor seven-day rules are reference only.

### G06

**Bosstiary slots and daily free change — M.** Equip/clear slots with durable reset-epoch allowance and atomic payment.

- **Events:** Bosstiary free boss-slot change
- **Dependencies:** Boss profiles, slot UI/commands, gold ledger and kill progress.
- **Donor versus new rules:** Both donors contain slot/fee rules.

### G07

**Bounty reroll interval qualification — S.** Source the interval and add any required rule change; do not implement an unreleased teaser.

- **Events:** Bounty Tasks daily reroll
- **Dependencies:** Existing token owner and a sourced/approved Global interval.
- **Donor versus new rules:** Crystal 20h; Canary 24h; current Global exact interval unverified.

### G08

**Regional Cyclopedia respawn competition — L.** Extend the deliberately limited one-area implementation with separate random/donation winners and stacking.

- **Events:** Cyclopedia Improved Respawn Rate
- **Dependencies:** Canonical map regions, donation ledger, home-spawn positions.
- **Donor versus new rules:** Crystal supplies partial area bonuses with different winner rules; Wiki needed.

### G09

**Close the existing W05b slice after #2781 — M.** Continuation of the existing owner only: qualify unresolved physical bindings and seasonal/full-moon details; do not duplicate its runtime.

- **Events:** Horse Station, Their Master's Voice, Fury Gates, Grimvale Moon / Full Moon, Nightmare Isles, Oriental Trader, Thawing
- **Dependencies:** Merge #2781 first; admitted items/NPCs/monsters, map overlays and horse mappings.
- **Donor versus new rules:** Crystal has executable fragments; gaps and accepted exclusions remain in #2781.

### G10

**Hive Born world stages — L.** Durable world totals, save-boundary stage changes and five-day success lifecycle.

- **Events:** Hive Born
- **Dependencies:** Hive stage maps, War Exp objectives, Hive boss/reward profiles.
- **Donor versus new rules:** No qualified shared controller; use Quest:Hive Wiki rules.

### G11

**Deeplings world stages — L.** World points, library/stair projection, five-save stage and personal access; unspecified threshold stays unavailable.

- **Events:** Deeplings
- **Dependencies:** Fiehonja map variants, coral/crystals, NPCs, three guardians and chest entitlements.
- **Donor versus new rules:** No complete shared controller; current Wiki plus sourced quest pieces.

**Demon Wars population balance — M.** Subtract opposite-faction kills and project the winning world state.

- **Events:** Demon Wars
- **Dependencies:** Faction creature IDs, kill owner, next-save spawn layouts.
- **Donor versus new rules:** No qualified shared controller; Wiki rules.

**Rotating tower and tomb access — M.** Shared phase clocks and access effects, not journal-only completion.

- **Dependencies:** Tower/tomb variants, guardian creatures, gates and board messages.

## 8. Questions for the owner / Left out

The binding discussion channel is [this draft PR #2798](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2798). Questions were posted as separate `QUESTION:` comments with options, donor/repository anchors and a recommendation. No `ANSWER:` comment had been received at the last audit read. These are open policy boundaries, not decisions attributed to the owner. They do not block the completed documentation census; a materially broader answer can require a follow-up census or historical/commercial implementation scope.

| Question | Options and recommendation | Discussion |
|---|---|---|
| Q1 — personal repeatable timers | A: named world/calendar/public rotations and shared recurring systems; internal character timers stay in their parent event. B: every repeatable quest/chest/boss timer as a separate row. **Recommend A**, used provisionally. Crystal First Dragon `actions_rewards.lua:48–68` and entrance `:60–87` demonstrate why internal24h/5d/365d timers should not multiply an annual event. | [QUESTION6559756252](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2798#issuecomment-6559756252) |
| Q2 — retired events | A: retain historical inventory without a default restoration backlog. B: optional historical compatibility. **Recommend A**. Easter replacement news2027 and Tournament news6638/7200 contrast with stale Crystal Store ticket/catalog entries `data/modules/scripts/gamestore/gamestore.lua:6621–6648`. | [QUESTION6559869360](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2798#issuecomment-6559869360) |
| Q3 — Store-only / real-money campaigns | A: Game owns earned/playable effects; advertising, sales windows and money settlement stay a separate Platform/commercial scope. B: add a cross-repository commercial-calendar backlog. **Recommend A**, retaining the boundary explicitly. Existing re-audit117,376–379 and Crystal `gamestore/init.lua:9–38` distinguish offer delivery from settlement; Halloween news3357 mixes gameplay and paid cosmetics without making each sale a world event. | [QUESTION6559872051](https://github.com/Oteryn/Oteryn-Game/pull/2798#issuecomment-6559872051) |

Unverified facts remain unverified: hidden rare-raid probabilities, New Year opening, some mini-change trigger thresholds, complete day/night actor admission, exact Global Bounty reroll interval and the unsourced Autumn producer values. No numeric default or project rule is invented to make a gap look complete. Where a donor value exists, the row distinguishes that implementation from verified current Global behavior. Future implementation must use a sourced rule or the owner's question channel.

## 9. Validation and limits

This PR changes only `docs/architecture/reviews/OTERYN_GAME_EVENTS_GAP_AUDIT_2026-11-01.md`. The existing re-audit, source code, content, vendor, CI and repository tooling remain untouched. The draft was opened immediately after the first documentation checkpoint commit; all rule/scope questions remain on the same PR.

The final document is assembled from the reviewed event records, with unique event names, one primary follow-up group per non-DONE current row,11-column event tables, exact count reconciliation and no pending mapping placeholders. The regional inventory reconciles119 headings. Donor XML references were checked against opened child bodies, and material live-main paths were independently reviewed. Documentation whitespace/diff and one-file publication checks are recorded in the PR body after execution. No cargo suite, client smoke or live gameplay test is claimed by this research-only change.

Completeness is bounded to the reconciled named calendar/world-change/public-rotation/regional-raid inventories and the explicitly reviewed donor candidates at the stated revisions. Hidden undocumented probabilities and owner-unsettled historical/commercial/personal-timer expansion remain visible limits. The report is a source-backed backlog, not a claim of runtime test coverage or an implementation of the listed missing mechanics.
